# Changelog

All notable changes to `core-agent` are recorded here. The format follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/) and the project follows [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## Stability promise

The SemVer-governed public API of `core-agent` is the exported surface of the `promised` packages in [`dev/api-surface.txt`](dev/api-surface.txt) (see [`docs/compatibility-policy.md`](docs/compatibility-policy.md)):

- `agent` (+ `agent/autonomous`, `agent/background`), `attach/wire`, `eventlog`, `tools`, `permissions`, `config`, `memory`, `models` (+ `models/anthropic`, `models/gemini`, `models/mock`, `models/profiles`), `pricing`, `recording`, `runner`, `transcript`, `usage`, `watchdog`, `instruction`, `mcp`, `skills`, `telemetry`

Remaining packages under `pkg/` (`agentenv`, `approval`, `approver`, `attach`, `attachadapter`, `auth`, `childenv`, `compose`, `digest`, `hooks`, `memory/memtest`, `modeltier`, `session`, `taskclass`, `tools/agentic`, `tools/alert`, `tools/peer`, `tools/vcs`) are marked `unsupported` in `dev/api-surface.txt`: they are exported for in-repo and sibling-tool composition (`cmd/`, `internal/`, `extras/`) and may change in minor releases (adapters under `extras/`, such as `extras/scion`, live in separate Go modules and are outside this module's stability promise). Incompatible changes to `promised` packages require a major version bump (`/vN`) and are gated on every PR by `dev/ci/presubmits/verify-apidiff` (and at release time by `dev/release/cut-ga-tag.sh`). Deprecated symbols carry a `Deprecated: ... Removed in v<N>.<M>.` marker enforced by `TestDeprecationMarkersNameTheirRemoval`.

---

## [Unreleased]

### Changes by Kind

#### Feature

- **Headless `--output-format=text|json|stream-json`, `-q` / `--quiet`, and TTY-gated `asst ›` prefix.** Omits `asst ›` when stdout is piped, adds JSON/NDJSON `-p` output in `pkg/runner`, and adds `--quiet` to suppress stderr summaries. ([#1379](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1379))
- **Subagent parent-input forwarding (`attach: ["input"]`, `include_input`, and `subagents[].inherit`).** Splices the parent turn's triggering message verbatim into sync and `spawn_agent` subagents without re-typing it. ([#1499](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1499))
- **Forge-neutral `vcs_*` tools (`pkg/tools/vcs`) and `config.vcs` for distroless GitOps.** Adds pure-Go `vcs_*` tools over `core-vcs@v1.1.0` (GitHub, GitLab, git), `builtin:propose-change`, token-file refusal, and `verify-core-vcs-pin`. ([#1490](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1490))
- **Multi-scope memory core (`pkg/memory`) and config schema (`pkg/config`).** Adds `Backend`, fan-out `Router`, `InMemory`, subagent scope derivation, `pkg/memory/memtest`, and `agent_engine` / `memory` config. ([#948](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/948))

#### Security

- **SHA-pin third-party GitHub Actions and digest-pin Dockerfile base images.** Pins all workflow `uses:` refs to 40-hex commit SHAs, digest-pins `Dockerfile` base images, adds `.github/dependabot.yml`, and enforces pins in CI. ([#1357](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1357))

#### Documentation

- **`examples/kode-gopher` recipe and site reference for Code Mode over MCP.** Adds a config-only `kode-gopher` recipe (`stdio` and Streamable HTTP `/mcp`), gated `execute_go_code` with `tool_notes`, and `/reference/kode-gopher/`. ([#1391](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1391))
- **Add `internal/evals/costbench` benchmark and qualify `5–10×` cost projections.** Benchmarks 4 configs via `pkg/pricing` and qualifies `5–10×` savings claims in docs. ([#1378](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1378))
- **Archive shipped/abandoned design docs to `docs/archive/` and rewrite `docs/north-star.md`.** Moves 88 non-proposed design docs to `docs/archive/`, records Goal 2 on `core-agent` + `core-tui`, and enforces `Status:` in `docs-lint`. ([#1388](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1388))
- **Clarify OTel/pricing outbound calls, prompt-cache read rates (`5–10%`), and CLI flag docs.** Updates `README.md`, `cmd/core-agent`, and `docs/site/` for `--no-pricing-refresh`, 5% cache reads, and `--config` / `--attach-register-*`. ([#1381](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1381))
- **`.worktrees/` worktree convention in `AGENTS.md` and `.gitignore`.** Documents isolating feature branches in `.worktrees/<name>` git worktrees and ignores `/.worktrees/` so parallel worktrees do not dirty the root checkout. ([#1116](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1116))

#### Other (Cleanup)

- **Extract attach protocol vocabulary into leaf package `pkg/attach/wire`.** Moves `pkg/agent` and `pkg/agent/background` wire types into `pkg/attach/wire` (re-exported in `pkg/attach` by alias) so `pkg/agent` drops `pkg/attach`. ([#1372](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1372))
- **Tighten complexity ratchet (`dev/tools/.golangci.yml` and `internal/testutil`).** Lowers `funlen.statements` to 142, separates `pkg/` (229) vs `dev/` (254) ceilings, ratchets `pkg/` >150-line count, and caps `cmd/core-agent` `run`. ([#1371](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1371))
- **Add `dev/tools/harness-ratio` LOC report and release budget.** Reports Harness vs Product non-test LOC in release notes and enforces `harness_added <= product_added` at release time. ([#1383](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1383))
- **`pkg/agent/autonomous`: extract shared `turnLoop` across `Run` and `Resume`.** Unifies the multi-turn execution, retry, schedule, and checkpoint loop and brings `Resume` into parity on `RunResult.Calls` provenance rollup. ([#1370](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1370))

## [3.1.0] — 2026-11-06

core-agent 3.1 completes the one-harness fold of `go-steer/mast` into `core-agent`: legacy `workload.yaml` bundles import or run directly via `core-agent config import-workload` and `agent.RunWorkload` / `compose.RunWorkload`, with specialist content roots (`.agents/roots/<name>/mcp.json`), declarative subagent modes (`chat`, `task`, `single_turn`), enforced `output_schema` and capability splits, and per-turn `agent.max_steps` / `final_report` budgets. For long-lived daemons, 3.1 ships the durable write gate (`pkg/approval` + recorded-effect outbox and SQL decision ledger), in-process scheduled triggers with single-fleet scheduling leases and deterministic pre-turn monitor collection (`monitor.collect` / `POST /monitor-ack`), bidirectional A2A v0.3 JSON-RPC 2.0 (`background.A2AClient` and `POST /a2a`), `attach.multi_session.auth.token_identity` + `proxy_unprovisioned`, and the Agent Substrate AX task image (`CORE_AGENT_GOOGLE_AUTH=injected` and `examples/deploy/ax/`). It also hardens multi-session isolation and security (loopback DNS-rebinding `Host`/`Origin` guard, daemon-admin gating for `yolo`/`bash`, bash verb profiles, per-session panic bulkheads, and durable call-time spend ledgers) and upgrades `core-models` to `v0.15.2` (adding `sap-ai-core`, `oauth2_client_credentials`, custom headers, and `pricing: "isolated"`). This is a backwards-compatible minor release on `github.com/go-steer/core-agent/v3` with zero breaking changes to promised packages (verified by `verify-apidiff` against `v3.0.0`), and all release artifacts now carry SLSA build provenance attestations.

### Changes by Kind

#### Feature
- **Session-witnessed substrate failure scenarios (`internal/evals/differentiators`).** Adds deterministic scenarios `E-runaway-loop-stopped`, `E-subagent-return-contract`, and `E-goal-survives-compaction` to `dev/tools/evals`. ([#966](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/966))
- **`attach.multi_session.auth.token_identity`: the attach token as a one-row bearer table.**
  - **What was missing:** `POST /sessions` is wired only in multi-session mode, which needed a `table_file` at mode `0600`. A daemon whose one client credential is its attach token, such as an AX task in serve mode, had nowhere to keep that file, so `POST /sessions` answered 501 ("no SessionFactory configured").
  - **What landed:** `token_identity` (mutually exclusive with `table_file`) makes `--attach-token` / `--attach-token-file` the table's one row, stored as its SHA-256, authenticating as that identity: sessions and ACLs work as with a table, and every turn is attributed to that identity. `compose.BuildMultiSessionAuthnWithToken` takes the token, `BuildMultiSessionAuthn` is unchanged, the startup summary reports `1 user (the attach token, as "<identity>")`, and the 501 names the setting. ([#1483](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1483))
  - **Post-merge follow-up:** `cmd/core-agent` now wires `SessionFactory` and `SessionResumer` whenever `--attach-token` / `--attach-token-file` is set even when `attach.multi_session.enabled` is false (so `examples/deploy/ax/task-serve.yaml` serves `POST /sessions` out of the box), `attach.multi_session.proxy_unprovisioned` (`BearerTokenAuth.WithUnprovisionedProxyCallers`, refused without `proxy_identities` and marked in the startup summary) allows a trusted proxy caller on either `table_file` or `token_identity` to assert downstream end-user identities via `X-Asserted-Caller` without pre-provisioning them in `users.json` (with no labels and no admin privileges on unprovisioned assertions, and `auth.ErrAssertedCallerInvalid` / `401` on malformed identities), `auth.LoadUsersFile` accepts any `token_sha256`-only `users.json` (`len(PlaintextIdentities()) == 0`) at mode `0644` while still rejecting group/world write/execute bits and requiring `0600` (or `0640` in an owning group) whenever any row carries a plaintext `token`, and `examples/deploy/ax/` pins the Substrate v1.0 AX runner (`12dbe16` / upstream `f0228f1`) and singular `spec.workspace` binding. ([#1485](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1485), [#1483](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1483))
- **A2A v0.3 JSON-RPC 2.0 server on the attach mux (`POST /a2a`), `config.A2AConfig`, and session-backed `compose.A2ABackend`.**
  - **What was missing:** `pkg/attach` served an A2A v0.3 Agent Card at `/.well-known/agent-card.json` and `pkg/agent/background` shipped an outbound `A2AClient` (`#1396`), but `core-agent` could not expose itself as an A2A v0.3 JSON-RPC 2.0 server on the attach listener (`go-steer/mast` carried `internal/a2a` and `internal/cli/a2a.go`).
  - **What landed:** `config.Config` gains `A2A A2AConfig` (`expose`, `skill_name`, `skill_description`, `input_schema`, `output_schema`, `auth.required`, `auth.scopes`, validated via `CompileJSONSchemaMap` and imported from `workload.yaml` via `WorkloadBundle.ParsedA2A()`). When `a2a.expose` is true, `pkg/attach` mounts `POST /a2a` (`message/send`, `message/stream` SSE with a 30s per-frame write deadline and pre-stream validation, `tasks/get`, and `tasks/cancel` with `A2A-Version: 0.3` header enforcement, 1 MiB body limit, per-skill scope checks, inbound `input_schema` validation, and cost rate-limiting on `message/send` and `message/stream` only) and updates `/.well-known/agent-card.json` to emit `"preferredTransport": "JSONRPC"`, `<external-url>/a2a`, and schema-enriched skill descriptions (`attach.RenderA2ASkillDescription`) while keeping the card 100% byte-identical when `a2a.expose` is false. `compose.A2ABackend` (`pkg/compose/a2a.go`, wired into `cmd/core-agent`) maps 1 A2A `contextId` to 1 `core-agent` session with per-caller ACL checks, bounds the in-memory task registry (`DefaultA2AMaxTasks = 1024`, `DefaultA2ATaskTTL = 24h`) while pinning in-flight tasks and preserving the cancel-wins invariant, maps P2 durable write-gate and input parks (`eventlog.ScanPendingParks`) to `"input-required"` (recoverable across daemon restarts from the durable session event log and resumable via `Agent.RunContent`), skips partial streaming events on SSE progress callbacks (`mast #408`), and runs `message/send` turns on a daemon-derived context (`mast #562`). ([#1407](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1407))
  - **Post-merge hardening:** `consumeA2ATurnEvents` observes events through `usage.TurnTap` and commits token usage and cost to `ag.Tracker()`, skips `p.Thought` in `extractContentText`, and returns the final non-partial assistant message text rather than concatenating intermediate tool-turn narration. `attach.a2aHandler` tracks the target skill per task (`rememberTaskSkill`) and enforces skill-level `Auth.Required` and `Auth.Scopes` on `tasks/get` and `tasks/cancel`, decodes requests with `dec.UseNumber()` to preserve 64-bit integer JSON-RPC IDs, and honours `closing` / `daemonCtx` by returning HTTP 503 with `Retry-After: 5` on `message/send` and `message/stream` during drain and cancelling in-flight SSE streams. `resumePersistedAgent` sets `deps.AutoContinueDeferInject = true` (and `withDeferAutoContinueInject`), `recoverTaskFromStore` queries `findPersistedSessionByTaskID` exclusively, `checkExistingTaskAccess` caches recovered state and skips redundant persistence scans on newly generated task/context IDs, `runPreTurn` skips destructive queue drains when `hasContent` is true (`RunContent`), and `validateJSONSchemaNode` / `validateA2AJSONSchemaNode` recursively validate all composite JSON Schema keywords. ([#1469](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1469))
- **Deterministic monitoring cycle (`compose.MonitorCollector`, `compose.Notifier`, `compose.MonitorAcker`), `POST /monitor-ack`, and `config.CheckMonitorCollectSurface`.**
  - **What was missing:** Scheduled monitoring daemons had no deterministic pre-turn collection hook (`monitor.collect`) to execute a read-only MCP tool before waking the model, no transition parser (`monitor.transitions_from` + `monitor.wake_on`) to skip quiet cycles with zero LLM calls, no outbound switchboard notifier (`monitor.notify` with `timeline` / `replace` update modes, `remind_after`, `digest_after` deadman heartbeat, and edge-triggered degraded alerts), no durable operator acknowledgement surface (`POST /monitor-ack` and `"mast-acks:mast-ops"`), and no config-load guard (`config.CheckMonitorCollectSurface`) preventing tool-calling subagents from invoking self-run monitor tools mid-turn.
  - **What landed:** `internal/monitor` adds `ParseResult` (`opened`, `remained`, `closed`, `re_opened`, `degraded`), `Transition`, and `Set`. `internal/notify` adds the outbound switchboard HTTP client (`New`, `Client`, `Post`, `Append`, `Replace`). `pkg/eventlog/ack.go` adds `AckRecord`, `(*Handle).RecordAck`, `(*Handle).Ack`, and `(*Handle).Acks` persisted on `"mast-acks:mast-ops"`. `pkg/config/monitor.go` adds `Config.Monitor` (`MonitorConfig`, `MonitorCollectConfig`, `MonitorNotifyConfig`, `MonitorAckConfig`) and `CheckMonitorCollectSurface`. `pkg/attach/monitorack.go` mounts `POST /monitor-ack` behind the attach auth middleware, deriving `By` strictly from the authenticated caller credential (`ErrMonitorAckAnonymous`, `ErrMonitorAckBadRequest`). `pkg/compose` adds `MonitorCollector`, `Notifier`, `MonitorAcker`, and `runOwnBehalf` (`pkg/mcp` adds `Unwrap() tool.Tool` on `digestingTool` so self-run collection bypasses digest wrapping), and `cmd/core-agent` wires them into daemon startup. ([#1406](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1406))
  - **Post-merge hardening:** `pkg/tools.gatedTool` and `pkg/tools.digestingTool` implement `Unwrap() adktool.Tool` so `compose.runOwnBehalf` peels outer permission-gate and digest wrappers on `monitor.collect` and `monitor.ack` calls; `internal/notify.Client` maps HTTP `409 Conflict` to `ErrSendFullText` and `POST` `404 Not Found` to `ErrNoSuchMessage`; `cmd/core-agent`'s `scheduledTurnWaiter` correlates scheduled wake-loop turns via `Agent.LastTurnDrainedID` / `LastTurnDrainedAny` / `LastTurnReachedDrain` / `InboxContainsID` / `RemoveInboxID` and concatenates multi-part `WakeLoop` text; `MonitorCollector` cleans up `"closed"` subjects before `wake_on` filtering and honors forwarded-ack, `re_opened`, and newer `Transition.ChangedAt` transitions; `Notifier` evaluates `remind_after` and `digest_after` against `Notifier.SetCycleTick`; and `MonitorAckConfig.Args`, `ScheduleConfig.EffectiveTimeout`, `ScheduledTriggerConfig.MaxFires` / `Timeout`, hyphenated MCP prefix normalization in `CheckMonitorCollectSurface`, and `monitor` / `schedule` preservation are wired across `pkg/config`, `pkg/compose`, `pkg/agent`, and `cmd/core-agent`. ([#1467](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1467))
- **In-process scheduled trigger (`compose.ScheduledTrigger`), single fleet scheduling lease (`compose.AcquireSchedulingLease`), boot auto-resume (`compose.AutoResumer`), and timed-pause rescan (`compose.PauseScheduler`).**
  - **What was missing:** `core-agent` had no in-process scheduled trigger (`schedule` config block in `.agents/config.json`) with a durable anchor (`eventlog.Handle.Schedule` / `SaveSchedule`) that preserves cadence across restarts and reports missed intervals as `skipped=N` on the next future lattice tick, no single fleet scheduling lease (`__mast_scheduling__`) preventing multi-replica double-fires, no `LeaseLost` cancellation of mid-flight scheduled turns, no ambiguous-effect block on `compose.AutoContinueStartupSessionWithConfig` when a mutating tool call lacked a `FunctionResponse` at crash time, and no periodic timed-pause rescan (`compose.PauseScheduler`) so timed pauses minted on passive replicas are armed by the lease holder.
  - **What landed:** `pkg/eventlog/schedule.go` and `pkg/eventlog/pause.go` add `ScheduleRecord` persistence on the reserved `"mast-scheduler:mast-ops"` session (`(*Handle).Schedule` / `SaveSchedule`) and `PauseRecord` persistence (`(*Handle).PauseGate`, `ScanPauses`, `FindToken`, `ConsumeToken`, `ConsumeScheduled`, `ExtendToken`). `pkg/compose/schedlease.go` adds `SchedulingLease` and `AcquireSchedulingLease` over `AcquireLock(ctx, appName, "_system", "__mast_scheduling__")` with a 32s stale-takeover retry window and fail-open fallback on store errors. `pkg/compose/schedtrigger.go` adds `ScheduledTrigger` (`NewScheduledTrigger`, `Seed`, `Run`, `Stop`, `ScheduledSessionID`, `ScheduledPayload`) with durable anchor alignment, missed-window `skipped=N` accounting, jitter clamping (`<= interval/2`), single-flight overlap skipping, `PreFire` gating, and immediate turn cancellation on `LeaseLost` or `Stop`. `pkg/compose/autoresume.go`, `pkg/compose/auto_continue.go`, and `pkg/compose/pausesched.go` add `AutoResumer` and `PauseScheduler` plus the ambiguous-effect block (`internal/effects.ScanDangling` + `Handle.EffectsAckedAt`) on `AutoContinueStartupSessionWithConfig` and `AutoContinueStartupRetryWithConfig`. `pkg/config` adds `Config.Schedule` (`ScheduleConfig`: `every`/`interval`, `jitter`, `prompt`) and `WorkloadBundle.ParsedSchedule()`, `cmd/core-agent` wires the schedule and effect guards into daemon startup, and `docs/scheduled-ops-design.md` records the settled decision (`#202`). ([#1405](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1405), [#202](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/202))
- **Terraform module for `examples/gke-platform-agent` (`examples/gke-platform-agent/deploy/terraform/`) and SLSA build provenance attestation (`release.yml`, `release-images.yml`, `dev/release/verify-provenance.sh`).**
  - **What was missing:** `examples/gke-platform-agent/scripts/grant-iam.sh` applied GKE Workload Identity Federation bindings imperatively via `gcloud`, which is additive-only and cannot declaratively remove an earlier `roles/container.admin` grant when narrowing a deployment to per-namespace RBAC remediation (`go-steer/mast#290`). Release archives and OCI container images were published without SLSA build provenance attestations (`actions/attest-build-provenance@v4`) or a post-publish provenance verifier.
  - **What landed:** `examples/gke-platform-agent/deploy/terraform/` adds the root Terraform module and `modules/wif` + `modules/release` submodules (tested offline via `terraform test` mock providers in `tests/*.tftest.hcl`, `examples/gke-platform-agent/terraform_test.go`, and `dev/ci/presubmits/verify-terraform`), binding additive `google_project_iam_member` roles (`roles/aiplatform.user`, `roles/mcp.toolUser`, and `roles/container.viewer` or `roles/container.admin`) so narrowing `write_scope` back to `"namespaced"` destroys `roles/container.admin`, while `modules/release` renders the namespace, `core-agent-gcp-env` ConfigMap, and per-namespace `Role`/`RoleBinding` matching `deploy/components/gated-apply/`. `.github/workflows/release.yml` and `.github/workflows/release-images.yml` generate SLSA build provenance attestations via `actions/attest-build-provenance@v4` and verify them with `dev/release/verify-provenance.sh`, and `dev/release/install-verify-footer.md.tmpl` documents `gh attestation verify` for both release archives and OCI images. ([#1408](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1408))
  - **Post-merge hardening:** `modules/wif` provisions `google_project_iam_custom_role.cluster_viewer` (`gkeAgentClusterViewer` with `container.pods.getLogs`), binds it alongside `roles/container.viewer` in `"namespaced"` scope, and enables `cloudtrace.googleapis.com` / `monitoring.googleapis.com` plus `roles/cloudtrace.user` (on both `core-agent-daemon` and `lookout-watch`) and `roles/monitoring.metricWriter` when `otel_enabled = true` (`terraform_test.go` verifies both `OTEL=0` and `OTEL=1` against `scripts/grant-iam.sh` without substituting `CUSTOM_ROLE`). Root `variables.tf` validates `project_id` and `write_scope`, `outputs.tf` passes full coordinates to `set-up-demo.sh` and `verify-gated-apply.sh`, `modules/release` merges decoded `extra_values` maps last-wins, `verify-terraform` strips local `.tfvars`/`.tfstate` files from its scratch copy, `readonly_grant_test.go` skips `.terraform` directories, `install-verify-footer.md.tmpl` and `verify-provenance.sh --oci` pin `--source-ref`, `verify-provenance.sh --local-dist` verifies every archive in `dist/checksums.txt`, and `release.yml` / `release-images.yml` check out `inputs.tag` and guard non-dry-run tag dispatches against mismatched `github.ref`. ([#1464](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1464))
- **`core-models` upgraded to `v0.15.2` (`github.com/go-steer/core-models` and `github.com/go-steer/core-models/adkv2`).**
  - **What was missing:** `core-agent` pinned `core-models v0.13.5`, predating `oauth2_client_credentials` authentication (`providers[].auth.client_id_env`, `client_secret_env`, `token_url`, `scopes`, `audience`, `client_auth`), per-profile custom request `headers` (`providers[].headers`), the SAP AI Core orchestration platform (`platform: sap-orchestration` and the built-in `sap-ai-core` profile), isolated backend pricing (`pricing: isolated`), and the 2026-11-06 LiteLLM built-in price catalog regeneration.
  - **What landed:** `go.mod` bumps `github.com/go-steer/core-models` to `v0.15.2` (and `github.com/go-steer/core-models/adkv2` to `v0.15.1`), `pricing.NewCatalog` receives `Profiles: cfg.Providers` across `cmd/core-agent`, `pkg/compose`, `pkg/usage`, and `internal/evals/evalcompose`, `Config.ProfileRates()` excludes isolated profiles from bare-key fallback, `Config.validateProviders()` rejects conflicting per-backend `pricing` modes at load time, and `pkg/config`'s `EnvRefs` inventory registers `providers[].auth.client_id_env` and `providers[].auth.client_secret_env`. ([#1411](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1411))
- **Trajectory, differentiator, LLM-judge, and live-cluster outcome eval suite (`internal/evals`, `internal/evals/differentiators`, `internal/evals/judge`, `internal/evals/harness`, `internal/evals/outcome`, `testdata/evals/`, and `testdata/outcome/`).**
  - **What was missing:** `core-agent`'s `internal/evals` held only a minimal rubric grader (`Rubric`, `Grade`, `RunBatch`), without `go-steer/mast`'s 31-scenario LangChain SRE incident corpus (`testdata/evals/scenarios/langchain-sre.jsonl` and `testdata/evals/intents.yaml`), 6-scenario differentiator suite (`internal/evals/differentiators/`), LLM-as-judge rig and graders (`internal/evals/judge/`), deterministic + live-judge harness (`internal/evals/harness/` and `internal/evals/cmd/evals`), or the 3-case (15-run) live-`kind` outcome evaluation suite (`internal/evals/outcome/`, `internal/evals/cmd/outcome`, and `testdata/outcome/`).
  - **What landed:** `internal/evals` ports the trajectory adapter (`Trace`, `Call`, `CallRecord`, `TraceFromEvents`, `RecordCalls`), dataset and intent-catalog loaders (`LoadDataset`, `LoadIntentTable`), deterministic and trajectory evaluators (`EvaluateAll`, `IntentCoverage`, `ToolCoverage`, `SeverityAccuracy`, `ExactlyOnce`, `EffectOrdering`, `ApprovalRequested`), corpus reachability and call validation checks (`CorpusReach`, `DeadMetrics`, `ValidateCalls`, `ClassifyMisses`), `internal/evals/differentiators` (6 scenarios: `E-exactly-once`, `E-ambiguous-refusal`, `E-budget-exhaustion`, `E-approval-rejected`, `E-approval-edited`, `E-feedback-capture`), `internal/evals/judge` (`NewRig`, `NewJudge`, `(*Judge).Grade`, `RunCost`, `MetricResponseQuality`), `internal/evals/harness` (`Run`, `LoadSummary`, `Summary.WriteText`, `Summary.WriteJSON`, `Summary.WriteDelta`), `internal/evals/outcome` (`Load`, `NewRunner`, `CreateCluster`, `NewProvisioner`, `NewSurface`, `Board`), and the `internal/evals/cmd/evals` and `internal/evals/cmd/outcome` CLIs alongside the existing `Rubric` / `RunBatch` surface. ([#1403](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1403))
- **One-call library facade (`agent.RunOneShot` / `agent.RunRecipe` / `agent.RunWorkload` and `compose.Run` / `compose.RunRecipe` / `compose.RunWorkload`) and `examples/embed/recipe`.**
  - **What was missing:** Embedding a recipe directory or a single-turn programmatic agent in a host Go binary required wiring `config.Load` → `models.Resolve` → `permissions.FromConfig` → `tools.GateToolset` → `agent.NewSubagentTool` → `usage.TurnTap` → `agent.New` by hand (as in `examples/embed/slim`), whereas `go-steer/mast` offered a top-level `mast.Run` / `mast.RunWorkload` entry point.
  - **What landed:** `pkg/agent` adds `OneShotConfig`, `OneShotResult`, `RunOneShot`, `RunRecipe`, and `RunWorkload` (`pkg/agent/run_facade.go`), which discover `.agents/config.json` and `.agents/subagents/*.md` from `Dir` (supporting both `<dir>/.agents/` and flat workload directories), read `AGENTS.md`, resolve the model and permission gate, wrap caller-supplied `Tools` and `Toolsets` via the new `tools.GateTool` / `tools.GateTools` and `tools.GateToolset`, materialize declared subagents (`mode`, `output_schema`, `tier`, `tools`, `budgets`), and return `Text`, parsed JSON `Output` (`map[string]any`), `SessionID`, and rolled-up `Usage` (`usage.Totals`). `pkg/compose` (`pkg/compose/run.go`) exposes `Run`, `RunRecipe`, and `RunWorkload` (with `RunConfig` and `Result` type aliases) that additionally expand `.agents/AGENTS.d/*.md` overlays and `@include` directives via `pkg/instruction`. `examples/embed/recipe` demonstrates `agent.RunRecipe` end to end and is covered by both `examples/internal/smokeset` and `dev/tools/verify-slim-deps`. ([#1404](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1404))
- **`core-agent config import-workload` converter (`config.LoadWorkload` / `config.ImportWorkload`) and `change-executor` specialist in `examples/gke-platform-agent/gated-apply/`.**
  - **What was missing:** Legacy `go-steer/mast` and `shipmind` workload bundles (`workload.yaml` + `specialists/*.specialist.md` + `schemas/*.json`) could not be converted mechanically into `core-agent` recipes (`.agents/config.json` + `.agents/subagents/*.md` + `.agents/schemas/*.json`), `config.ToolsConfig` had no schema for per-tool mutation/precondition/capture policies (`tools.policies`), and `examples/gke-platform-agent/gated-apply/` did not yet carry `mast`'s `change-executor` specialist and `change-report.json` output schema.
  - **What landed:** `config.LoadWorkload` and `config.ImportWorkload` (`pkg/config/workload.go`), exposed on the CLI as `core-agent config import-workload <workload-dir> [--out <recipe-dir>]`, parse and validate `workload.yaml` bundles with strict `KnownFields(true)` decoding, enforce the capability-split invariant between `change_executor` and read-only diagnoser specialists, reject retired `dispatch: graph` and `planner.enabled: true` configurations with clear guidance, map `tool_catalog.tools` to `permissions.allow` (`mcp:<server>_<tool>*` and `spawn_agent:<specialist>`) and `tools.policies` (`ToolPolicyConfig`, `ToolPreconditionConfig`, `ToolCaptureConfig`, `ToolRevertConfig`), map `hitl` / `hitl_policy` to `permissions.mode`, map `budget` (`max_cost_usd`, `max_turns`, `max_wallclock_seconds`, `final_report`), `safety.watchdog`, `builtin_tools` (baseline-off), `prompt_cache`, and `mode`, write `.agents/subagents/<name>.md` and `.agents/schemas/*.json`, and validate the generated recipe via `config.Load`. `examples/gke-platform-agent/gated-apply/` adopts `change-executor` (`.agents/subagents/change-executor.md`, scoped to the four GKE tools the `gated-apply` MCP surface exposes) and `.agents/schemas/change-report.json`. ([#1402](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1402))
- **Declarative subagent execution modes (`chat` / `task` / `single_turn`), enforced `output_schema`, `tier` resolution, `capability` checks, per-tool `mcp_tools` allowlists, and `.agents/subagents/<name>.md` authoring.**
  - **What was missing:** `config.SubagentSpec` only ran multi-turn conversational (`chat`) subagents returning unstructured text, required pinning a provider-specific `model` block rather than a capability `tier`, could not filter individual tools within a shared MCP server on a per-subagent basis without starting a separate server, had no startup check verifying that a read-only specialist held only read-only tools, and required inlining subagent definitions in `config.json` (or standing up a full `root` directory) rather than authoring single-file `.agents/subagents/<name>.md` specialists (`go-steer/mast` carried these in `pkg/specialists` and `internal/agent`).
  - **What landed:** `config.SubagentSpec` gains `Mode` (`"chat"` default in JSON, `"task"`, `"single_turn"`), `OutputSchema` (relative `.json`/`.yaml`/`.yml` path confined to the `.agents/` scope root via `config.LoadOutputSchema`, or inline JSON schema via `agent.ParseOutputSchema`), `Tier` (`"small"`, `"mid"`, `"frontier"`, resolved via `Config.ResolveTierModel` and mutually exclusive with `Model`), `Capability` (`"read_only"` — enforced at startup across built-in and MCP tools via `tools.IsReadOnlyTool` — and `"change_executor"`), and `MCPTools` (`[]MCPToolAllowlist`, wrapping server toolsets with `mcp.FilterToolset`). `pkg/agent` adds `NewTaskAgent`, `NewSingleTurnAgent`, `NewCoordinator`, `FinishOnStall`, `RefuseOnGate`, `WithSubagentMode`, and `WithOutputSchema` (on ADK `v2.6.0`'s `workflowagent`): in `task` mode the subagent terminates via `finish_task` (whose parameter schema carries `OutputSchema` and rejects non-conforming JSON so the model can self-correct) and in `single_turn` mode runs one toolless call with `ResponseSchema` + post-call validation; `NewSubagentTool` returns both `result` (string) and parsed `output` (`map[string]any`) for `task`/`single_turn` while keeping `chat`-mode tool declarations byte-identical (`TestNewSubagentTool_ModeUnsetIsChat`). `config.Load` discovers `.agents/subagents/*.md` files with strict `KnownFields(true)` YAML frontmatter (`mode:` required), rejects retired `{key?}` / `{artifact.foo?}` state-injection placeholders in the Markdown body, and marks Markdown-sourced specs so `config.Save` never copies them into `config.json`. ([#1401](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1401))
- **Durable write gate (`pkg/approval`), recorded-effect outbox (`internal/effects`), durable approval ledger (`eventlog.DecisionStore`), and attach park/ack projection (`pkg/attach`).**
  - **What was missing:** `core-agent` gated mutating tool calls in-memory (`pkg/permissions` and `pkg/approver`), without a crash-recoverable ADK confirmation pause (`adk_request_confirmation`), pre-mutation state capture and revert derivation, a durable outbox that detects mutating tool calls that dispatched before a crash (`FunctionCall` with no matching `FunctionResponse`), a durable SQL decision ledger (`agent_approval_decisions`), or attach endpoints to inspect open confirmation parks and acknowledge dangling effects. In `go-steer/mast` (`#561`), the ADK confirmation placeholder `FunctionResponse` (marked via `ev.Actions.RequestedToolConfirmations[fr.ID]`) closed the original `FunctionCall` in `pairScan` before the operator approved it, and `BeforeToolCallback` persisted the `Decision` row only in `EventActions().StateDelta` *after* the tool returned, so a crash mid-execution after confirmation replay left zero durable trace and allowed a duplicate execution on resume.
  - **What landed:** `pkg/permissions` gains `ToolClass` (`ClassMutating`, `ClassReadOnly`, `ClassSpawning`), `MutatingMode`, `MutatingDecision`, `DefaultToolClass`, `MCPToolClass`, and `Gate.CheckMutatingToolCall` / `RecordMutationVerdict`. `internal/effects` provides the session-log outbox scanner (`ScanDangling`, `IsConfirmationApproved`, `SubRunStateKey`, `DescribeSubRunPark`) with the `#561` `pairScan` fix: confirmation placeholder responses keep `open[origID]` with `excluded = true` until `adk_request_confirmation` resolves, and synthetic tail-repair events (`CustomMetadata["core_agent_synthetic_repair"] == true`, stamped by `pkg/agent/tail_repair.go`, which also skips `LongRunningToolIDs`) are ignored so tail repair never hides an unconfirmed or crashed mutating call. `pkg/approval` ports the ADK confirmation plugin (`NewPlugin`, `WithDecisionSink`, `WithDecisionLookup`, `Verdict`, `DecodeVerdict`, `CaptureRecord`, `DescribeConfirmation`, `DescribeCapture`, `DescribeChangeSet`) with synchronous fail-closed pre-execution decision persistence via `DecisionSink` and a per-`FunctionCallID` duplicate-dispatch guard (`dispatched` + `DecisionLookup`). `pkg/eventlog` adds `DecisionStore` (`agent_approval_decisions`, `RecordDecision`, `HasDecision`, `ListDecisions`), `AckEffects` / `EffectsAckedAt`, `ScanPendingParks`, `ListParks`, and `ExportDecisions`. `pkg/agent` adds `WithPlugins` to wire ADK plugins into `runner.New`, and `pkg/attach` serves `GET /parks`, `GET /parks/{session}`, `GET /sessions/{id}/parks`, `POST /ack-effects`, and `POST /sessions/{id}/ack-effects` while detaching turn execution onto a daemon-owned context (`#562`). ([#1400](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1400))
- **Built-in A2A v0.3 `RemoteAgentSpawner` (`background.A2AClient`) and declarative `subagents[].remote` routing (`spawn_agent { agent: "<name>" }`).**
  - **What was missing:** `pkg/agent/background` defined the `RemoteAgentSpawner` interface and `spawn_remote_agent` tool, and `pkg/attach` served an A2A v0.3 agent card at `/.well-known/agent-card.json`, but `core-agent` shipped no concrete `RemoteAgentSpawner` implementation and no way to declare an A2A v0.3 remote delegate in `subagents[]` and route `spawn_agent { agent: "<name>" }` to it (`go-steer/mast` carried an A2A v0.3 client in `pkg/subagent/a2a.go`, though only for synchronous `message/send` + `tasks/get` polling).
  - **What landed:** `background.A2AClient` (`pkg/agent/background/a2a_client.go`, `a2a_config.go`, `a2a_wire.go`) implements `RemoteAgentSpawner` over A2A v0.3 JSON-RPC 2.0: it fetches and caches `/.well-known/agent-card.json`, verifies JSON-RPC transport and declared skill allowlists, injects Bearer tokens from `auth.token_env` and W3C `traceparent` headers from the active OpenTelemetry span, streams `message/stream` Server-Sent Events (`status-update` and `artifact-update`) into `RemoteAgentEvent` alerts when `capabilities.streaming` is true, falls back to `message/send` + `tasks/get` polling when streaming is not advertised, and sends `tasks/cancel` on `Stop`, context cancellation, or `MaxWallclock` expiry. `config.SubagentSpec` gains `Remote *SubagentRemoteConfig` (`a2a`, `endpoint`, `skill`, `skills`, `auth`, `timeout_seconds`), `background.SubagentTemplate` gains `Remote RemoteAgentSpawner`, and `SpawnTemplate` (`spawn_agent { agent: "<name>" }`, both `wait: true` and `wait: false`) routes remote templates through `launchRemote` + `fanInRemote` so one `spawn_agent` tool surface dispatches both in-process and remote A2A subagents. ([#1396](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1396))
- **Public API compatibility gate (`dev/tools/verify-apidiff`), promised-surface manifest (`dev/api-surface.txt`), and v4.0 deprecation discipline (`docs/compatibility-policy.md`).**
  - **What was missing:** `CHANGELOG.md` named a prose list of supported packages (which still described pre-1.0 `v0.X` rules and omitted `agent/autonomous`, `agent/background`, and `watchdog`), nothing in CI ran `apidiff` against the latest GA tag (`v3.0.0`) or required new `pkg/` packages to be classified as promised versus composition-only, and 33 `Deprecated:` markers across `pkg/` lacked a concrete `Removed in v<N>.<M>.` target version.
  - **What landed:** `dev/api-surface.txt` classifies all 37 importable `pkg/` packages into 22 `promised` packages and 15 `unsupported` composition packages, enforced on every `go test` run by `internal/testutil.TestEveryImportablePackageIsADecision`. `dev/tools/verify-apidiff` (and `dev/ci/presubmits/verify-apidiff`) compares every `promised` package against the nearest `v[1-9]*` GA tag with `golang.org/x/exp/cmd/apidiff` and fails on any unacknowledged incompatible change (unless recorded in `dev/api-breaks.txt` or a `**BREAKING:**` bullet in `CHANGELOG.md` whose line names both the package and the symbol in backticks — a bare-word match was tried first and one bullet mentioning `config.json` or "core-agent." acknowledged every later break in the package). All 33 `Deprecated:` comments across `pkg/` now end with `Removed in v4.0.`, enforced by `internal/testutil.TestDeprecationMarkersNameTheirRemoval`, and `docs/compatibility-policy.md` documents the compatibility policy and v4.0 removal schedule. The published Library API page states the promised/unsupported split and marks `pkg/compose` and `pkg/session` as unsupported where it documents them. ([#1368](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1368), [#1373](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1373), [#1398](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1398))
- **Reference slim-embed consumer (`examples/embed/slim`) and CI dependency-hygiene gate (`dev/ci/presubmits/verify-slim-deps`).**
  - **What was missing:** Decision 9 of `docs/mast-fold-design.md` retired the separate "mast as a slim library" option because `core-agent`'s runtime governance slice (`pkg/agent`, `pkg/models`, `pkg/permissions`, `pkg/tools.GateToolset`, and `pkg/usage`) was already free of the interactive TUI (`core-tui`, `charmbracelet`), the MCP SDK, Prometheus, the OpenTelemetry SDK/exporters, and SQLite drivers — but nothing in CI enforced that import boundary or exercised the minimal embedding path end to end.
  - **What landed:** `examples/embed/slim` wires a `core-models` `llm.LLM` (adapted via `pkg/models.Adapt`), `permissions.Gate` in `ModeAllow`, `tools.GateToolset`, `usage.Tracker` + `usage.TurnTap` + `usage.PriceFor`, and `pkg/agent` in `ModeAutonomous`, registered as a runnable target in `examples/internal/smokeset` (`embed/slim`). `dev/tools/verify-slim-deps` (and its CI delegator `dev/ci/presubmits/verify-slim-deps`) checks `go list -deps ./examples/embed/slim` against the slim-embed denylist (`cmd/*`, `internal/{attachclient,coretuievent,coretuiremote,evals,webui}`, `pkg/{approver,attachadapter,compose,instruction,mcp,runner,skills,telemetry}`, `core-tui`, `charmbracelet`, `modelcontextprotocol`, `prometheus`, `go.opentelemetry.io/otel/{sdk,exporters}`, and SQLite drivers). ([#1397](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1397))
- **`core-agent` can now run inside an Agent Substrate sandbox as an AX task without holding Google credentials in the sandbox (`CORE_AGENT_GOOGLE_AUTH=injected` and `examples/deploy/ax/`).**
  - **What was missing:** In an Agent Substrate sandbox (`github.com/agent-substrate/substrate`), outbound credentials are injected by the egress gateway, which only replaces an `Authorization` header that is already present on the wire. Without a placeholder header, `core-agent`'s Vertex AI providers (`vertex`, `anthropic-vertex`, and `google_adc` profiles such as `vertex-maas`) and HTTP MCP servers with `auth.google_oauth` failed at startup looking for Application Default Credentials (`google.FindDefaultCredentials` / `core-models/auth.GoogleADC`). In addition, `go-steer/mast` carried the three-binary AX task image (`ax-task-runner`, the golden-boot wrapper, and the agent binary) and the one-shot and serve-mode task manifests, while `core-agent` had only the historical `axplore` design notes.
  - **What landed:** Setting `CORE_AGENT_GOOGLE_AUTH=injected` (`pkg/agentenv.InjectedGoogleAuth`, wired into `pkg/models.ResolveProfileOptions`, `pkg/models.InjectedGoogleCredential`, `pkg/models/gemini`, `pkg/models/anthropic`, `pkg/models/profiles`, and `pkg/mcp`) skips ADC discovery and sends `Authorization: Bearer placeholder` so the egress gateway can substitute the real token. `examples/deploy/ax/` ships the multi-stage `Dockerfile` (`/usr/local/bin/ax-task-runner` pinned by `.ax-fork-ref` / `.ax-ref`, `/usr/local/bin/ax-agent-entry`, and `/usr/local/bin/core-agent` with the `gke-platform-agent` recipe), `entrypoint/main.go` (`--wait-for-egress`, `--state-dir=/workspace/.core-agent`, `done` marker and `result.txt` tee), `task.yaml` (one-shot), `task-serve.yaml` (long-lived attach server with `CORE_AGENT_GOOGLE_AUTH=injected`), and `README.md` covering golden boot, full versus data-only snapshots, CPU-platform pinning, and `--parked-request-budget`.
  - **Decisions and rejected alternatives:** `injectedauth` lives in `pkg/agentenv` (with `core-models` bridging in `pkg/models`) rather than a new top-level `pkg/` package or `go-steer/purser`, keeping the exported package list unchanged ahead of `#1398` and avoiding blocking `shipmind`'s AX cutover on `purser` Phase 2 (`go-steer/purser#34`). Weekly drift detection (`.github/workflows/ax-pin-check.yml` and `dev/ax-pin-check`) compares `google/ax` `main` against `.ax-ref` and `mastersingh24/ax` against `.ax-fork-ref` and opens or updates one rolling issue rather than failing CI. Also fixed a boundary check in `examples/internal/recipecheck`'s `deployRoot` walk, which previously walked onto `examples/deploy` for recipes without their own `deploy/` directory.
  - **Deliberately not changed:** ADC discovery when `CORE_AGENT_GOOGLE_AUTH` is unset or set to any value other than `"injected"`, and the `gke-platform-agent` Kubernetes manifests under `examples/gke-platform-agent/deploy/`. ([#1395](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1395), [#1410](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1410))

#### Bug or Regression
- **Allow Substrate router `Host` headers in `examples/deploy/ax`.** Merges `config.ax.json` (`attach.allowed_hosts`) into the image's recipe config so `ax-task-runner`'s pass-through does not 421. ([#1497](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1497))
- **Scope imported workload MCP servers to specialist content roots (`pkg/config`, `pkg/compose`).** `ImportWorkload` writes per-specialist `.agents/roots/<name>/mcp.json` instead of a main-agent `.agents/mcp.json`. ([#1498](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1498))
- **Enforce `agent.max_steps` per-turn cap and `final_report` (`pkg/agent`, `pkg/compose`, `cmd/core-agent`).** `WithMaxSteps` and `WithFinalReport` bound each turn's tool-call cycles and optionally grant one tool-less synthesis call. ([#1500](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1500))
- **Consult `RemoteAgentHandle.Status` on remote stream close and cap terminal handles (`pkg/agent/background`).** `fanInRemote` checks `Status()` on unexpected stream close and `Manager` caps terminal handles at `64`. ([#1366](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1366))
- **Skip busy sessions during multi-session idle eviction (`pkg/attach`, `pkg/compose`).** `SessionRegistry.EvictBefore` checks `entryBusy` so active, deferred, or subagent-running sessions are never evicted mid-turn. ([#1363](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1363))
- **Default unattended `ask` mode approval timeout to 30m (`cmd/core-agent`).** Applies `DefaultUnattendedApprovalTimeout` (`30m`) when running unattended with an attach prompt surface so unanswered prompts time out cleanly. ([#1364](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1364))
- **Per-session panic bulkheads (`pkg/agent`, `pkg/runner`).** Recovers panics in `Agent.runTurn`, `runner.WakeLoop`, and `background.Manager.Spawn` so one panicking turn never crashes sibling sessions. ([#1365](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1365))
- **Per-session `agentic_*` context binding (`pkg/agent`, `pkg/tools/agentic`, `pkg/compose`).** `Agent.runTurn` stamps the active `*agent.Agent` onto context so `agentic_*` and digest fallbacks resolve the calling tenant. ([#1362](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1362))
- **Skip `agentic_*` registration when no cheap-tier model exists (`cmd/core-agent`).** `resolveAgenticTools` skips `agentic_*` wrappers when `models.ResolveSmallModel` returns `""` and logs the reason. ([#1377](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1377))
- **Compact before long-context pricing cliffs (`pkg/agent`).** `DefaultCompactor.TriggerFor` triggers at the lower of `tier × window` and `long_context.threshold_tokens - 32k` to avoid 2–5× surcharge tiers. ([#1376](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1376))
- **Bound single-row `session.Service.Get` calls with `NumRecentEvents: 1` (`pkg/eventlog`, `pkg/agent`, `pkg/attach`).** Eliminates O(N) full-history reloads across 18 append and state-read call sites. ([#1367](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1367))
- **Fix `TestSessionACLStore_ConcurrentPut` SQLite lock-upgrade flake (`pkg/attach`).** Configures `newTestACLStore` with `_txlock=immediate` and WAL mode to match `eventlog.Open`. ([#520](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/520))
- **Fix `TestSubagentTool_WallclockBudgetStopsTheDelegation` scheduling flake (`pkg/agent`).** Asserts `elapsed >= childDelay` (`2s`) rather than a 1s goroutine scheduling bound. ([#1029](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1029))
- **Fix attach listener bind deadline and `PromptBroker` test sleeps (`pkg/attach`).** Hoists listener bind deadlines and replaces `time.Sleep(20ms)` with `waitForPending` polling. ([#1386](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1386))
- **`workload.yaml` importer (`config.ImportWorkload`, `config.LoadWorkloadConfig`) and runtime enforcement of `tools.policies`, `max_wallclock_seconds`, and `final_report`.**
  - **What was broken:** Follow-up to `#1402`: `ToolPolicyConfig` did not deserialize `park_hint_fields` from `workload.yaml` or `.agents/config.json`; `permissions.FromConfig` and `approval.New` ignored `cfg.Tools.Policies` (`mutating`, `park_hint_fields`, `precondition`, and `capture` were parsed into `Config.Tools.Policies` and never wired into `permissions.Gate` or `approval.Config`); `Config.Agent.MaxWallclockSeconds` was never wired into `agent.New`, `agent.RunOneShot`/`RunWorkload`, `compose.Run`/`ReproduceAgent`, or `cmd/core-agent`; `Config.Agent.FinalReport` was parsed into config but never wired into `SubagentBudgets` / `agent.NewSubagentTool` at runtime; `agent.RunWorkload` and `compose.RunWorkload` required pre-running `import-workload` and ignored `spec.MCPTools` per-tool filtering in `pkg/agent/run_facade.go`; `ImportWorkload` did not copy `<workloadDir>/coordinator.md` into `AGENTS.md`, did not prune stale `.agents/subagents/*.md` files or refresh generated `AGENTS.md` on re-import, allowed symlinks inside `schemas/`, emitted `spawn_agent:<tool>` allow patterns for any hyphenated MCP tool name, and did not sanitize MCP server prefixes (`mcp.sanitizePrefix`) in `permissions.allow`; and `Config.Validate` did not validate `Tools.Policies` or reject negative `MaxWallclockSeconds`.
  - **What landed:** `ToolPolicyConfig` adds `ParkHintFields []string` (`park_hint_fields`), validated by `Config.Validate` alongside `Precondition`, `Capture`, and `MaxWallclockSeconds >= 0` (with fatal typo detection on `tools.policies`). `permissions.FromConfig` wires `cfg.Tools.Policies` into `Gate.SetToolClasses` and `Gate.ToolClass` falls back across `<server>_` MCP prefixes for non-built-in tools. `pkg/approval` adds `WithToolPolicies`, `MutatingFromPolicies`, `ParkHintFieldsFromPolicies`, `PreconditionFromPolicies`, and `CaptureFromPolicies` (qualifying `Precondition.Read`, `Capture.Read`, and `Capture.Revert.Call` with the invoked MCP server prefix) plus `Config.ParkHintFields` in `writeGate.hintKey`. `pkg/agent` adds `WithMaxWallclock` and `SubagentBudgets.FinalReport` (`subagentFinalReportGate`), wired across `cmd/core-agent`, `pkg/compose`, and `pkg/agent/run_facade.go`. `config.LoadWorkloadConfig` loads a `workload.yaml` bundle directly into `*Config` so `agent.RunWorkload` and `compose.RunWorkload` run un-imported workload directories (including `coordinator.md` and `spec.MCPTools` filtering), and `ImportWorkload` copies `coordinator.md`, refreshes generated `AGENTS.md`, prunes stale `.agents/subagents/*.md` files, rejects `schemas/` symlinks, restricts `spawn_agent:` allow rules to declared specialists, and sanitizes MCP server prefixes. ([#1455](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1455))
- **Declarative subagent execution modes (`mode`, `output_schema`, `tier`, `capability`, `mcp_tools`, and `.agents/subagents/*.md`) now discover Markdown subagents under `-c` / `--agents-dir`, preserve read-only tool classification through digest and timing wrappers, enforce `enum` and `anyOf` constraints in `output_schema` at runtime, and report stalled `task` subagents as `no_return` (`Returned = false`).**
  - **What was broken:** Follow-up to `#1401`: `config.LoadFile` bypassed `.agents/subagents/*.md` discovery and `cmd/core-agent`'s `loadConfig` did not re-run Markdown discovery when `--agents-dir` overrode the initial `-c` directory (`Config.ApplyAgentsDir` did not exist); `digestingTool.ReadOnlyHint`, `timedTool.ReadOnlyHint`, and `gatedTool.ReadOnlyHint` returned `false` when wrapping built-in read-only tools (`grep`, `glob`, `list_dir`, `read_many_files`, `retrieve_raw`, `agentic_*`) that do not directly implement `ReadOnlyHinter`, causing `capability: "read_only"` subagents to fail startup when CCR digesting was active (`--no-digest=false`) and `WritesSharedFilesystem` to classify wrapped survey tools as writers; `SubagentSpec.Validate` accepted `mode: "single_turn"` specs with non-empty `tools`, `mcp`, `mcp_tools`, or `skills` lists and did not require `mcp_tools[].server` to appear in explicit `mcp` lists; `resolveSubagentToolsets` silently produced an empty toolset when every `mcp_tools[].tools` pattern on a server was a typo; `resolveSubagentProvider` passed `""` instead of `parentProvider.Name()` into `ResolveTierModel` when `cfg.Model.Provider` was omitted; `expandInlineSubagentInstruction` resolved Markdown `@include` targets against `projectRoot` instead of `.agents/subagents/` and skipped post-expansion `{placeholder?}` checks; `loadSubagentOutputSchema` fell back to `projectRoot` after a scope-escape error on `spec.BaseDir()`; `agent.ParseOutputSchema` rejected `.yaml`/`.yml` schemas and bypassed scope containment; ADK's runtime validator ignored `enum` and `anyOf` constraints and accepted empty `single_turn` responses; and `autonomous.Run` treated `FinishOnStall`'s synthetic `finish_task` completion as `Returned = true` (`StopReason = "natural"`) and recorded `finish_task` in `RunResult.Calls`.
  - **What landed:** `Config.ApplyAgentsDir` reloads `.agents/subagents/*.md` idempotently (called by `Load`, `LoadFileWithAgentsDir`, and `cmd/core-agent`'s `loadConfig` after `--agents-dir` resolution), `isFatalUnknownKey` fails closed on unknown keys inside `subagents[]` and `mcp_tools[]`, and `splitSubagentFrontmatter` handles empty frontmatter (`---\n---`) and indented `---` lines inside multiline YAML scalars. `digestingTool`, `timedTool`, `gatedTool`, and `serializedTool` implement `Unwrap() tool.Tool`, `digestingTool`/`timedTool`/`gatedTool.ReadOnlyHint` delegate to `IsReadOnlyTool(inner)`, `readOnlyBuiltins` and `nonFilesystemMutators` include `retrieve_raw` and `agentic_*`, and `agentic` wrappers implement `ReadOnlyHint() bool { return true }`. `SubagentSpec.Validate` rejects tools/MCP/skills on `single_turn` specs and requires `mcp_tools` servers to match explicit `mcp` lists; `resolveSubagentToolsets` fails closed when a configured `mcp_tools` entry matches zero tools on a live server; `resolveSubagentProvider` falls back to `parentProvider.Name()`; `expandInlineSubagentInstruction` resolves Markdown `@include` relative to `spec.BaseDir()` and runs `config.CheckSubagentPlaceholders` on expanded text; `loadSubagentOutputSchema` and `agent.ParseOutputSchema` enforce scope-root containment and `.yaml`/`.yml` parity; `agent.ValidateOutputValue` enforces `enum`, `anyOf`, required fields, and unknown properties across `BeforeToolCallbacks` (`finish_task`), `NewSubagentTool`, and `autonomous.Run` (rejecting empty `single_turn` replies when `OutputSchema != nil`); and `autonomous.Run` unwraps ADK's `{"result": ...}` wrapper, recognizes `agent.Stalled` outputs as `Returned = false` (`StopNoReturn`), and excludes `finish_task` from `driverTools` provenance. ([#1450](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1450))
- **Guardrail store pre-run operator resets, atomic budget extensions, and schema documentation (`pkg/agent`, `pkg/attachadapter`, `pkg/eventlog`, `docs/site`).**
  - **What was broken:** Calling `RecordGuardrailReset` or `AttachGuardrails` / `AttachResetGuardrail` on a freshly started process before the session's first `Run` wrote a new `agent_guardrail_state` row before `RestoreGuardrails` had run, causing a subsequent `RestoreGuardrails` to skip the pre-upgrade legacy eventlog fold or double-count the granted budget extension; `RestoreGuardrails` silently discarded `SaveState` backfill errors; `addBudgetExtensionOnDB` used a read-modify-write instead of an atomic SQL increment; and the published session/embed docs omitted the `agent_guardrail_state`, `agent_budget_spend`, `agent_run_lock`, `agent_continuation_claim`, `agent_boot_log`, and `agent_approval_decisions` tables.
  - **What changed:** `(*Agent).RecordGuardrailReset`, `(*Adapter).AttachGuardrails`, and `(*Adapter).AttachResetGuardrail` restore persisted guardrail state before reading or writing guardrail resets; `RestoreGuardrails` logs `slog.Warn` on legacy backfill `SaveState` errors; `addBudgetExtensionOnDB` increments `budget_extension_usd` atomically in SQL (`budget_extension_usd = budget_extension_usd + ?`); `TestGuardrailEventContractMatchesEventLogStore` locks the guardrail event contract between `pkg/attach` and `pkg/eventlog`; and `concepts/sessions.md` and `embed/api.md` document all tables managed by `eventlog.Open`. ([#1439](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1439))
- **Session-tree spend rebuild and `:sub:` child cleanup (`pkg/usage`, `pkg/compose`, `pkg/eventlog`, `pkg/agent/internal/subsession`).** Parent turns whose provider stamps a served `ModelVersion` distinct from `Model.Name()` (for example `claude-opus-4-6-20260601`) now keep the session's pinned `pricingRate` on event-stream rebuild (`restoreResumedTracker` and `resolveRebuildPricing`), child-branch tool responses (`ev.Branch != ""`) no longer replay MCP digest `savings` sidecars onto the parent tracker (`RebuildTrackerFromEvents`), `sessionHydrator` prefetches distinct sessions in parallel (`maxHydrateConcurrency = 8`) when a query spans multiple `:sub:` child sessions, `Service.Delete` cleans up `:sub:` child sessions across `agent_eventlog`, `agent_guardrail_state`, `agent_budget_spend`, `agent_approval_decisions`, and ADK `sessions`/`events`, and `BranchInjectingService` shares the `usage.*MetadataKey` constants. ([#1434](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1434))
- **Harden `Handle.claimSync` terminal-window race, document remote A2A spend accounting, and expand A2A v0.3 test coverage.**
  - **What went wrong:** `(*Handle).claimSync` rejected claims when `h.status != StatusRunning`, leaving a window between status transition (`terminalAlertText` / `finalize` / `StopAndReport`) and `takeSyncClaim()` where a synchronous `awaitResult` waiter could fail to claim the handle and allow a duplicate completion alert into the background inbox, while `takeSyncClaim()` did not mark `h.sync` when no claim was present; `docs/site/src/content/docs/reference/configuration.md` did not state that remote A2A v0.3 delegates do not report token usage or model turn counts toward local `max_turns` or `max_cost_usd` budgets; and `pkg/agent/background/a2a_test.go` lacked mutex-guarded `fakeA2AServer` field reads, deterministic polling-cancellation synchronization, and unit tests for malformed agent card JSON, streaming JSON-RPC error frames, `Stop` during polling, and `input-required` on `Spawn`.
  - **What was fixed:** `(*Handle).claimSync` now relies on `<-h.done` and `h.sync == syncNone` (with `takeSyncClaim()` transitioning unclaimed handles to `syncPassed`) so synchronous waiters arriving before `takeSyncClaim()` always suppress duplicate inbox alerts and waiters arriving after `takeSyncClaim()` never assume suppression; `configuration.md` documents remote A2A budget and spend semantics; and `a2a_test.go` adds mutex-safe `fakeA2AServer` accessors, deterministic polling cancellation synchronization, and tests for the remaining error and cancellation paths. ([#1437](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1437))
- **Scheduled trigger, pause scheduler, auto-resume, scheduling lease, and daemon effect-control hardening (`pkg/compose`, `pkg/eventlog`, `pkg/config`, `cmd/core-agent`).**
  - **What was broken:** `buildDaemonEffectControls` constructed `effects.NewPredicate(overrides)` without MCP `ReadOnlyHint` annotations or unprefixed `tools.policies` suffix matching on MCP-prefixed tool names, treating read-only MCP calls as ambiguous mutating calls in `AutoResumer` and `auto_continue`; `PauseScheduler.fireDue` relied solely on `ps.fire` to consume timed pauses and did not call `Handle.ConsumeScheduled` or ignore `ErrAlreadyResumed`/`ErrTokenNotFound`, causing `RunRescan` to re-fire timed pauses every rescan pass; `AutoResumer.appendRepair` did not stamp `effects.SyntheticRepairMetadataKey` on synthetic repair events (`ScanDangling` still flagged repaired calls as dangling) or re-evaluate sessions ending in a synthetic repair event when `resume()` failed; `ScheduledTrigger.persist` dropped `LastFire`, allowed non-monotonic `Fires`, and `seed` did not persist updated `Interval` values; `Handle.PauseGate`, `PauseInterrupt`, `consumeToken`, and `ExtendToken` had a TOCTOU read-modify-write race under concurrent consumption; multi-session daemons did not start `ScheduledTrigger` or isolate scheduled ticks onto per-tick sessions; `buildConfigFromWorkload` ignored `ParsedSchedule()` / `ParsedA2A()` errors; `AcquireLock` and `acquireSchedulingLeaseWithin` did not retry transient delete/contention races; and `auto_continue` read only a 64-event tail window in `hasUnackedAmbiguousEffects` and incremented the boot circuit breaker before checking for ambiguous effects.
  - **What changed:** `buildDaemonEffectControls` wires MCP `ReadOnlyHint` annotations via `effects.NewPredicateWithHints` and matches unprefixed `tools.policies` overrides against MCP-prefixed tool names; `PauseScheduler.fireDue` ignores `ErrAlreadyResumed`/`ErrTokenNotFound` and calls `Handle.ConsumeScheduled` after `ps.fire` succeeds; `AutoResumer.appendRepair` stamps `SyntheticRepairMetadataKey`, is idempotent when a synthetic repair event is already trailing, and `lastEventIsUnhandledCall` skips trailing synthetic repair events so failed `resume()` calls retry on the next boot; `ScheduledTrigger` tracks `lastFire`, preserves monotonic `Fires` and `LastFire` in `persist`, and persists updated `Interval` on `seed`; `eventlog.Handle` serializes pause state mutations under `pauseMu` and bounds `ScanPauses`/`FindToken` `Service.Get` calls with `NumRecentEvents: 1`; multi-session daemons run `ScheduledTrigger` with per-tick session isolation (`fire.SessionID`) and shared `LeaseLost` cancellation; `buildConfigFromWorkload` propagates `ParsedSchedule()` and `ParsedA2A()` errors; `AcquireLock` and `acquireSchedulingLeaseWithin` retry transient contention races; and `auto_continue` re-fetches full session events when the tail window is saturated and checks `hasUnackedAmbiguousEffects` before `RecordBoot` and `Registry.Lookup`. ([#1466](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1466))
- **A2A v0.3 remote handles (`background.A2AClient`) no longer block `Stop()` or `Manager.Close()` on network I/O, properly assemble multi-line SSE frames and streaming `artifact-update` chunks, bound buffered alerts with drop-oldest eviction, and cancel remote tasks on stream/poll abandonment.**
  - **What was broken:** Follow-up to `#1396`: `a2aHandle.Stop` performed a synchronous `tasks/cancel` HTTP round-trip inside `Stop()` with a 5s timeout, blocking `Manager.Stop` and causing `Manager.Close`'s 5s drain window to time out when stopping multiple remote tasks against an unresponsive server; calling `Stop()` after a task had already finished also sent a spurious `tasks/cancel`, and an unread 32-element `h.events` buffer blocked `emitAlert` / `emitTerminal` and leaked `h.run`. On the streaming path (`message/stream`), `runStream` dispatched on every single `data:` line instead of accumulating consecutive `data:` lines until a blank line per the WHATWG SSE specification, appended every `artifact-update` event as a separate `A2AArtifact` (causing `extractTaskResult` to join streamed token chunks with `"\n"` and allowing unbounded memory growth), and failed to send `tasks/cancel` when abandoning a stream on malformed JSON or non-terminal `Final: true` status updates. On the polling path (`tasks/get`), a single transient 5xx or transport hiccup aborted a long-running task with zero retries and no poll jitter, and `c.call` / `handleStreamFrame` did not verify JSON-RPC 2.0 response `id` matching.
  - **What landed:** `a2aHandle.Stop` now updates handle state and cancels `runCtx` immediately without network I/O while `h.run` issues `sendCancelOnce` (`a2aCancelTimeout = 3s`) as it unwinds; `markRemoteTerminal` suppresses `tasks/cancel` once the remote task has reached a terminal state, and `pushEvent` uses non-blocking drop-oldest eviction so `h.run` never blocks or leaks when `Events()` is not drained. `pkg/agent/background/a2a_stream.go` adds WHATWG-compliant `scanSSEFrames` (`maxA2ASSEFrameBytes = 8 MiB`), merges `artifact-update` chunks by `ArtifactID` (`append: true` concatenates text directly onto the last text part; `append: false` replaces in-place) under `a2aMaxStreamArtifactBytes` (8 MiB) and `a2aMaxStreamArtifacts` (256), and calls `abandonFailed` (`sendCancelOnce` + `finishFailed`) on every client-side stream or poll abandonment path. `pollTaskToTerminal` and `pollHandleLoop` add ±20% poll jitter (`pollDelay`) and retry transient HTTP 5xx/408/429 and `ErrRemoteUnreachable` errors up to `maxA2ATransientPollErrors` (3) consecutive times before canceling and failing, and `checkRPCResponseID` enforces JSON-RPC 2.0 `id` matching. ([#1436](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1436))
- **Out-of-band guardrail and audit drains no longer wedge forever behind a poison event or post-ADK partial failure, and `GuardrailStore.LoadWithLegacyCheck` now seeds a zero sentinel row and probes `agent_eventlog` via a composite `(app_name, user_id, session_id, author)` index.**
  - **What was broken:** Follow-up to `#1360`: `drainOutOfBandEvents` (`pkg/agent/guardrail_persist.go`) re-queued `pending[i:]` on the first `AppendEvent` error with no retry cap and no duplicate-key recovery. On the non-hook fallback path (or when a transport/wrapper failed after ADK committed the `events` row), retrying the same `ev.ID` hit a deterministic primary-key violation on `events(id, app_name, user_id, session_id)` forever, blocking every guardrail trip, reset, and audit row queued behind it. Separately, `GuardrailStore.LoadWithLegacyCheck` (`pkg/eventlog/guardrail.go`) ran on every never-tripped session because nothing wrote a zero sentinel row, and its `WHERE app_name = ? AND user_id = ? AND session_id = ? AND author IN (...) LIMIT 1` query walked all session rows in `agent_eventlog` via `idx_agent_eventlog_session` (`O(events in session)`) rather than `O(1)`.
  - **What landed:** `drainOutOfBandEvents` wraps queued entries in `{ev, attempts}`, treats duplicate primary-key collisions on `events` as already committed (while `eventlog`'s non-hook fallback path heals any missing `agent_eventlog` and `agent_guardrail_state` writes idempotently on retry), and drops permanently failing events with `slog.Error` after `maxOutOfBandEventAttempts` (3) attempts so healthy rows behind them still flush. `eventlog.Open` adds covering composite index `idx_agent_eventlog_session_author` on `(app_name, user_id, session_id, author)`, `Service.Create` seeds a zero `agent_guardrail_state` row (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) for newly created sessions, and `LoadWithLegacyCheck` seeds the sentinel row after a negative legacy probe on existing pre-upgrade sessions so subsequent loads and restores hit the primary key directly in `O(1)`. ([#1438](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1438))
- **`config.Load` now fails closed on unknown keys across `safety`, `url_scope`, `path_scope`, `attach`, `builtin_tools`, `subagents[].remote`, and tool-restriction typos, and aligns unknown-key traversal and `config.Save` with `encoding/json`'s case-insensitive struct matching.**
  - **What was broken:** Follow-up to `#1375`: `isFatalUnknownKey` only rejected unknown keys under `permissions.*`, `pricing.*`, `attach.cost_rate_limit.*`, and `.budgets.*`. Typos in adjacent safety, scope, lockdown, remote-delegation, and tool-restriction blocks — such as `safety.watchdgo`, `url_scope.denny`, `path_scope.allow_path`, `attach.read_only` (the field tag is `readonly`), `attach.multi_session.auth.tabel_file`, `model.builtin_tools.websearch`, `tool.disable`, `tools.disabled`, `subagents[0].tool`, `subagents[0].mcp_servers`, or `subagents[0].remote.auht` — emitted only a stderr warning while failing open. In addition, `structFieldsByJSONKey` and `marshalForSave` indexed `json:` tags case-sensitively whereas `encoding/json` matches struct fields via Unicode simple case-folding (`{"Permissions":{"denny":["bash(rm *)"]}}` or `{"Attach":{"Cost_Rate_Limit":{"brust":2}}}` unmarshaled the degraded struct without entering the fatal nested check, and `Save` could duplicate or drop mutations on mixed-case top-level keys), `strictProviders` inspected only the last `"providers"` entry when duplicate keys merged slice elements, and raw substring matching on `"usd"` / `"mtok"` false-positived on words such as `"statusd"`.
  - **What landed:** `walkUnknownStructKeys` and `marshalForSave` now mirror `encoding/json` (exact `json:` tag match first, then `strings.EqualFold` normalized to the canonical key), `strictProviders` validates every top-level `providers` block regardless of casing or duplication, `isFatalUnknownKey` fails closed across `safety.*`, `url_scope.*`, `path_scope.*`, `attach.*`, `.builtin_tools.*`, `.remote.*`, and container/tool-restriction typos while matching `"usd"` and `"mtok"` on word/camelCase/digit boundaries, and `docs/site/src/content/docs/reference/configuration.md`, `docs/site/src/content/docs/concepts/providers.md`, and `BuiltinToolsConfig` document the unknown-key rules accurately. ([#1442](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1442))
- **A `/pricing/refresh` or `/pricing/set` between a session's eviction and its resume changed the session's historical cost and moved the point where `--max-session-cost-usd` trips, because the spend ledger shipped for `#1359` was never written to or read from.**
  - **What was broken:** `eventlog.SpendStore` (`agent_budget_spend`) landed for the `#1359` design row "per-call spend ledger priced at call time, restored on resume; never re-priced", but no caller invoked `Append`, `Fold`, `FoldTree` or `History` outside its own tests. `compose.ReproduceAgent`'s `origin == "resumed"` branch still rebuilt the per-session `usage.Tracker` from the event stream via `usage.RebuildTrackerFromEvents`, pricing every historical turn with `usage.PriceFor(model, deps.Cfg)` — the catalog in force at *resume* time. A session billed $X under one catalog came back reporting a different total under the next, and the session ceiling tripped early or late accordingly.
  - **What landed:** the write path is one hook, not one call per billing site: `usage.Tracker.SetOnTurn(func(Turn))` fires for every `AppendUsage` **and** `AppendSideUsage` (unlike `SetOnAppend`, which skips side calls), and `compose.ReproduceAgent` installs one per multi-session session (after any resume restore completes) that appends each turn — model, backend, the six token buckets, the cost as billed, `Unpriced`, `Side`, `At` — to `Handle.Spend` under the root session id, synchronously (one `INSERT` under a detached 10s context so turns finishing during daemon shutdown drain still persist), logging failures with `slog.Warn` and never failing the turn. Every billing site that touches the session's `usage.Tracker` (`pkg/runner`'s wake loop; `pkg/agent`'s subagent, subtask, approver, savings-sidecar and compaction/side-question paths; and `pkg/agent/autonomous` for `spawn_agent` runs) is covered without a signature change. The ledger is idempotent on the ADK event: `usage.TurnUsage.EventID` is stamped by `TurnTap.Commit` (and as `<event_id>:savings:<i>` on MCP digest savings sidecars), carried onto `usage.Turn.EventID`, and stored in a new nullable `event_id` column (`size:191`) under a unique index on `(app_name, user_id, session_id, event_id)` with `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`; calls with no event at call time (the approver, internal compaction/side-question calls) store a `NULL` key so same-tick calls never falsely collide. On resume, `ReproduceAgent` reads `SpendStore.HistoryTree` first and, when the tree has any row, seeds the tracker through the new `usage.Tracker.RestoreTurns` — cost, `At`, `Side` and event id verbatim, no callbacks, no re-pricing — so `Totals`, `TotalsByModel`, `Turns` and `Last` match the evicted tracker exactly; the session's stderr line now says `spend restored from ledger (N turns)` or `from events`. `AutoMigrate` adds the columns and index to a pre-existing `agent_budget_spend` table in place.
  - **Decisions and rejected alternatives:** a ledger write at each `AppendUsage` call site was rejected: the tracker is the one place every billed turn already passes through, and a hook there cannot miss the next site. `event_id` is nullable rather than defaulted so the unique index can be created over an existing table and a key-less row is never mistaken for a duplicate. The event-stream fallback stays for trees with no ledger rows, and once `RebuildTrackerFromEvents` succeeds in full it seeds the ledger atomically in one transaction via `SpendStore.AppendBatch` at the resume-time price — the only price known for those turns — so a mid-stream failure never commits a truncated ledger and every later resume takes the ledger path. The ledger is read as rows (`HistoryTree`), not as a `FoldTree` aggregate, because a tracker holds its turns in memory live and `Last()` needs the last conversation turn, not a sum.
  - **Deliberately not changed:** `RebuildTrackerFromEvents` itself and its per-model pricing; `SpendStore.Fold`/`FoldTree`, whose `ByAuthor` key now carries the model name for rows the hook writes; the `SetOnAppend` contract (still conversation turns only, still not fired on restore); `usage.DigestSavings`, which the ledger does not replay. The earlier `#1359` entry below described `SpendStore` as providing durable per-call accounting; it provided the table. ([#1433](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1433), [#1359](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1359))
- **`config.Load` now walks nested config blocks recursively for unknown JSON keys, warning on non-critical unknown paths and failing closed on typos under `permissions.*` or cost/budget ceilings.**
  - **What was broken:** `warnUnknownKeys` (`pkg/config/discovery.go`) inspected only top-level keys of `.agents/config.json`. A mistyped nested key such as `{"permissions":{"denny":["bash(rm *)"]}}` or `{"agent":{"max_sesion_cost_usd":1}}` produced no warning and was silently dropped by `encoding/json`, leaving the deny rule absent or the session cost ceiling at the $10 default.
  - **What landed:** `checkUnknownKeys` walks the raw JSON config recursively against the reflection-derived `json:` schema of `config.Config` (descending through nested structs, slices of structs, and struct-valued maps while allowing dynamic keys on scalar/freeform maps). Unknown keys outside safety-critical paths emit a one-line warning naming the full dotted/indexed path (`model.vertex.context_cache.unknown_knob`, `subagents[0].future_knob`), while unknown keys under `permissions.*` or targeting cost/budget ceilings (`agent.max_sesion_cost_usd`, `subagents[0].budgets.*`, `attach.cost_rate_limit.*`, `model.pricing.*`) return a fatal load error. ([#1375](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1375))
- **Resumed sessions now rebuild subagent and subtask spend across the full session tree (`eventlog.WithSessionTree`) with per-model pricing, and `eventlog.SpendStore` (`agent_budget_spend`) persists a per-call spend ledger.**
  - **What was broken:** On lazy session resume (`compose.ReproduceAgent` with `origin == "resumed"`), `RebuildTrackerFromEvents` scanned `eventlog.ForSession(app, user, sid)` and passed a constant `pricingFor` returning the parent model's `pricingRate`. Delegated (`WithSubagents`) and background (`spawn_agent`) subagents write their events into child sessions (`<sid>:sub:<branch>:<inv>`), so `ForSession` dropped every subagent turn on resume; even when included, a constant `pricingFor` priced cheaper flash-tier subagents at parent model rates, and ADK's database serialization dropped `LLMResponse.ModelVersion`.
  - **What landed:** `compose.ReproduceAgent` now queries `eventlog.WithSessionTree` on resume and resolves per-model rates via `usage.PriceFor(model, deps.Cfg)`. `eventlog` and `subsession.BranchInjectingService` stamp `CustomMetadata["model_version"]` (plus `billing_backend` and `skip_parent_usage`) so `usage.RebuildTrackerFromEvents` recovers each turn's model across database persistence, skips `SkipParentUsage` subtasks (replaying tool-response `"savings"` sidecars instead), and records `"sub.*"` subtask branches via `AppendSideUsage`. `eventlog.SpendStore` (`pkg/eventlog/spend.go`, wired as `Handle.Spend`) also provides an append-only `agent_budget_spend` table with `Append`, `Fold`, `FoldTree`, and `History` for durable per-call spend accounting. ([#1359](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1359))
- **Out-of-band guardrail and audit drains now bound session reads (`NumRecentEvents: 1`), re-queue un-appended events on transient database errors, and persist guardrail state in `eventlog.GuardrailStore` (`agent_guardrail_state`).**
  - **What was broken:** `drainOutOfBandEvents` (`pkg/agent/guardrail_persist.go`), `drainInterruptAudit` (`pkg/agent/interrupt_audit.go`), `drainRefusalStormAudit` (`pkg/agent/refusal_storm.go`), and `turnApprover.drainAudits` (`pkg/agent/approver_context.go`) called `Service.Get` with `NumRecentEvents: 0`, hydrating the entire session event history just to obtain a `session.Session` handle for `AppendEvent`, and silently discarded queued guardrail trip/reset and audit rows whenever `Service.Get` or `Service.AppendEvent` failed. `RestoreGuardrails` likewise hydrated and folded the entire session event history on every restore.
  - **What landed:** `eventlog.GuardrailStore` (`pkg/eventlog/guardrail.go`, wired as `Handle.Guardrail`) maintains durable per-session watchdog trip, cost-ceiling trip, and budget-extension state in `agent_guardrail_state` so `RestoreGuardrails` reads current guardrail state by primary key without hydrating the session history (with sentinel seeding and composite-index legacy probing completed in `#1438` above). `drainOutOfBandEvents` requests `NumRecentEvents: 1` on `Service.Get`, logs `Get`/`AppendEvent` failures with `slog.Warn`, and re-queues any unwritten rows at the front of `pendingOutOfBandEvents` for the next drain window; `drainInterruptAudit`, `drainRefusalStormAudit`, and `turnApprover.drainAudits` now route through `queueOutOfBandEvent` and flush in one post-turn pass. ([#1360](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1360))
- **The weekly `ax-pin-check` rolling issue could report a wrong upstream distance, silently fall back to the pin as "current", and be filed twice.** Four gaps in the first cut of `dev/ax-pin-check` (#1410). The figure rendered as "`google/ax:main` is N ahead of `.ax-ref`" was `upstream.ahead_by + patch_stack.behind_by`, which double-counts upstream commits the fork has already merged. The head SHA was read off the compare payload's `commits[]` — GitHub documents that list as capped at 250 with the tip last *on an unpaged call*, a pagination property rather than a contract — and when that list was empty the code fell back to the pin itself, so a report could name `.ax-ref` as "main" and read as "nothing moved". The truncation flag only looked at the 300-file ceiling, not at `total_commits` versus the commits listed. And the workflow found the existing rolling issue by title *search*, which is eventually consistent and breaks on a rename, so a slow index would open a duplicate. Now the upstream-ahead figure is `ahead_by` alone and the mislabelled `fork_behind_upstream` field is gone; each branch tip comes from `GET /repos/{repo}/commits/heads/main` and a failed lookup is an error, never the pin; a compare listing fewer commits than `total_commits` is truncated; the response bound is 64 MiB with an explicit error at the limit (compare payloads carry a diff per file); and the workflow finds the rolling issue by the `ax-pin-check` label (created idempotently) plus the body marker it already wrote, null-safe in jq. Deliberately not changed: the third compare (`.ax-ref...main` inside the fork) still errors rather than reporting when the fork lacks the upstream pin; the header comment now says why. `dev/README.md`'s scheduled-automation table gains the `ax-pin-check` row it was missing. ([#1418](https://github.com/go-steer/core-agent/issues/1418))
- **The examples smoke gate could be bypassed by nesting: `examples/deploy/ax/entrypoint/main.go` was a program under `examples/` that CI neither ran nor had excluded with a reason, and the accounting test passed.** `smokeset.Discover` looked one level down, then grew an `embed/<name>` special case (#1397), and the AX entrypoint (#1395) landed two levels down between the two.
